Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in stratigraphy theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 469 and the invariant I(A,B) = 813 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 469 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stratigraphy structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 813. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
